| thread-recall-en | en | **fail** | talks about sessions; missing /index/i; missing /await\|wait/i; no memory_search; no search of past conversations; judge: The partner never states the recall-test fix (the test raced the index build and now awaits indexReady() before its first query). | thread-recall-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner answers in short, warm Chinese as someone who remembers: the 11-12 Codex session, the race between the test and index build, and the fix of awaiting indexReady() before the first query. ## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 폴더의 깨진 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 코드 작업을 부탁하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 그때 이어서 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 좋아요. 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くまで確実に届ける」、UDPは「速さ優先で、届かなくても気にしない」が一番の違いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu rama privada sobre la base y dejar la historia lineal, siempre antes de compartirla. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar tal cual cuándo y cómo se integró el trabajo, porque reescribir commits que otros ya tienen causa problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接的：发之前先建立连接，丢了会重发，保证按顺序到达；UDP 则不管这些，尽快发出去，快但不保证送到。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local, unpushed branch to tidy it into a clean line before it goes anywhere. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rewriting shared history breaks everyone else's copy.
```

A progress report with `asks` ticks off the ones already done while you go on.

  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径放在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

如果不想写完整路径，`bun test foo` 会运行路径里包含 `foo` 的测试文件。

要只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change is written down before it's applied to the actual data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed changes survive and half-finished ones can be cleaned up.

- 改动提交在你现在的分支上，不要合并，不要推送，不要开新的 PR，也不要改 PR 的状态。

完成的标准：锁粒度按 review 意见改好，测试通过，报告里写清改了哪几处、为什么这样改够细、测试输出。

```

(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Making the board now, filled with the two nights I have.
  [partner message] Done. The board is up with nights 2 and 3 filled in, and each new nightly result will add a row.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2188 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Writes per second, p99 latency</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="runs"></div>
  </div>
  <script>
    function render() {
      var runs = ducter.get('runs') || [];
      var list = document.getElementById('runs');
      list.innerHTML = '';
      if (!runs.length) {
        var e = document.createElement('div');
        e.className = 'k-empty';
        e.textContent = 'Nightly results will list here.';
        list.appendChild(e);
        return;
      }
      var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
      document.getElementById('latest').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
      document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent =
        'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
      runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
        var row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'k-row';
        row.innerHTML =
          '<div class="k-row-main">' + r.night + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · stalls ' + r.stalls + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-value">' + r.writes + ' /s</div>';
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    ducter.onChange(render);
    render();
  </script>
</main>
<style>
  .k-app { font-family: inherit; color: var(--d-ink); background: var(--d-bg); padding: 16px; }

  .k-card { background: var(--d-card); border: 1px solid var(--d-line); border-radius: 12px; padding: 4px 12px; }
  .k-row { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr auto; gap: 2px 8px; padding: 10px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--d-line); }
  .k-row:last-child { border-bottom: none; }
  .k-row-main { font-weight: 600; }
  .k-row-sub { grid-column: 1; color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 13px; }
  .k-row-value { grid-column: 2; grid-row: 1 / span 2; align-self: center; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .k-empty { color: var(--d-muted); padding: 12px 0; }

  ducter.onChange(fetchAndLoad);

})();

                      '<div class="k-row-value">' + f.ml + ' ml</div>';
      row.onclick = function(){
        ducter.ask('删掉 ' + f.at.slice(11,16) + ' 的 ' + f.ml + 'ml 这一条？').then(function(a){
          if (a && a !== 'no' && a !== false) {
            feeds = feeds.filter(function(x){ return x.id !== f.id; });
            save();
          }
        });
      };
      box.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  $('add').onclick = function(){
    var m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec($('t').value.trim());
    var ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
    if (!m || !(ml > 0)) return;
    var at = today() + 'T' + pad(+m[1]) + ':' + m[2];
    feeds.push({ id: Date.now(), at: at, ml: ml });
    $('ml').value = '';
    $('t').value = '';
    $('add').closest('details').open = false;
    save();
  };

  feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
  ducter.onChange(function(){ feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || []; render(); });
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"at":"2026-11-20T06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"at":"2026-11-20T09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs. Tap each one when it lands, and the count of waiting ones drops as you go.
  [an app card under the message: made "Waiting PRs", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1746 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting on someone</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">3</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub">Tap each one when it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Your PRs</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<style>
.pr{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;width:100%;text-align:left;background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:8px;color:var(--d-ink);font:inherit;cursor:pointer}
.box{flex:none;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid var(--d-muted);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:13px;color:#fff}
.pr[aria-pressed="true"] .box{background:var(--d-good);border-color:var(--d-good)}
.pr[aria-pressed="true"] .t{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
.t{font-weight:600}
.s{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted);margin-top:2px}
</style>
<script>
const PRS=ducter.get("prs") || [];
let done={};
function render(){
  const list=document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML='';
  let waiting=0;
  PRS.forEach(p=>{
    const on=!!done[p.id];
    if(!on && !p.draft) waiting++;
    const b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='pr';
    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',on);
    b.innerHTML='<span class="box">'+(on?'✓':'')+'</span><span><div class="t">'+p.title+'</div><div class="s">'+p.repo+' · '+p.status+'</div></span>';
    b.onclick=()=>{done[p.id]=!done[p.id]; ducter.set('landed',done); render();};
    list.appendChild(b);
  });
  document.getElementById('count').textContent=waiting;
  ducter.summary(waiting+' waiting');
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v=>{done=v||{}; render();});
ducter.onChange(v=>{done=v||{}; render();});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"d412","title":"Compaction cut","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #412","status":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"m10287","title":"Retry on 502","repo":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","status":"CI is failing"},{"id":"h1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","status":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"d418","title":"Apps bar","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #418","status":"Draft, not waiting on anyone","draft":true}]}

```
